> **(B7, found by the gate) Histograms are NOT left alone.** This section said "metaslab histograms until B10 flushes
> (OpenZFS itself lets histograms drift, `metaslab.c:4469-4476`)". OpenZFS's drift is DOWNWARD only — it allocates only
> in a loaded metaslab, whose histogram it rewrites from the range trees at every sync, and adds a free's segments
> unmerged to an unloaded one — and `metaslab_load_impl` ASSERTs it (`metaslab.c:2670`: the segment weight read from
> the histogram never above the loaded one's). agnos's allocations made it drift UP: the `wr` lane's B5 write (20 MiB
> over the three metaslabs the B7 payload's fixture build gave a space map) left bucket 23 claimed where bucket 20 was
> real, and the verify's C-zdbm — zdb `-mmmm` after OpenZFS's own round trip and export — aborted on it. Every txg now
> LOWERS the histogram of each metaslab it allocated in (k.68).

### e.5 What is refused rather than maintained

Livelists (clones), bookmark FBN (datasets with bookmarks), checkpoint space maps, DDT/BRT refcounts (deferred, never
touched), feature activations of any kind (no zstd, no sha512/skein/edonr/blake3 unless already active on the dataset,
no > 128 KiB blocks unless `large_blocks` is active on it, no multi-slot dnodes, no `longname`, no `large_microzap`), MOS
feature changes (so labels never change).

## (f) ZPL and ZAP mutations

### f.1 Object allocation and SA

First-fit free slot (512 zero bytes, `dn_type == 0`, not inside a previous dnode's `dn_extra_slots`) scanning the
meta-dnode's L0 blocks via their fills; extend `maxblkid` with a zeroed 16 KiB block when none is free. One slot always
(D14 — review fix: was "D15"). New dnode per WPA § 5 (SA bonus → `nblkptr` 1, checksum/compress inherit, `USED_BYTES|USERUSED|USEROBJUSED`).
SA: attribute numbers looked up **by name** in REGISTRY at mount — **(review fix)** an attribute the bulk list needs
that REGISTRY does not contain yet (OpenZFS registers lazily on first use, e.g. `PROJID` in a dataset that never had
a project id) → refuse the create by name rather than register it (registration is a REGISTRY ZAP add in the same
txg; not built); the bulk list `MODE SIZE GEN UID GID PARENT FLAGS ATIME
MTIME CTIME CRTIME LINKS [PROJID] DACL_COUNT DACL_ACES [SYMLINK]`; **reuse the LAYOUTS entry with the identical array**
(loaded whole at mount, the reader already does); if none matches, add one (`count + 2`, decimal key, BE u16 array) in
the same txg — fat-ZAP add, so it needs B8 (until then: refuse the create, named). PROJID + `ZFS_PROJID` when the
dataset has project quota enabled — **(B6 review)** exactly `dmu_objset_projectquota_enabled`: `project_quota` enabled
on the pool AND the projectused dnode open, which `dmu_objset_open_impl` (`dmu_objset.c:520-537`) guarantees by growing
a smaller objset_phys to 4 KiB IN MEMORY; agnos models the 4 KiB-on-disk case (`dse +104` bit 4) and refuses a create
where the growth would happen (bit 64), not "mirror the root directory's flags" (the fixtures' roots carry no
`ZFS_PROJID`, their later files do); PROJINHERIT copies the parent's projid and flag.
GEN = T; times = `ntp_now()` seconds + tick-derived nanoseconds, or the ub timestamp when the clock was never seeded.
Trivial ACL from the mode (`zfs_acl_chmod`: 3 ACEs for 0644/0755, up to 6 for odd modes; WPA § 10). **(B6 review)**
the masks are `acl_trivial_access_masks`' (`acl_common.c:1543-1600`): every class `0x120088`, the owner also `0xC0110`
(base `0x1E0198` whatever its bits), r `0x1`, w `0x6` (WRITE_DATA | APPEND_DATA, every class), x `0x20`; agnos builds the
3-entry form only and refuses by name a mode that needs allow0 / deny1 / deny2 (`zfs_wc_trivok`: a group bit the owner
lacks, an other bit the group lacks) — unreachable while the ABI has no mode argument (k.55).
> **(B6, as built — `zfs_write.cyr` § "B6")** `zfs_wc_slot` is the first fit as written: the meta-dnode's L0 blocks
> dirty-first (`zfs_wf_bp` down the dirty indirects), a block whose BP fill says full skipped unread, a hole read as
> all free, `dn_extra_slots` stepped over; with none free, block `dn_maxblkid + 1`, which grows (`zfs_wf_levels` where
> `dn_nlevels` would have to — never on a 6-level ZPL meta-dnode). OpenZFS's own cursor spreads objects over 128-object
> chunks (`dmu_object_alloc_impl`), so the fixtures' datasets have holes and free slots in every block and a create
> reuses the lowest free number. The REGISTRY numbers are read at each CREATE, not at mount (`zfs_wc_regs`: one walk of
> a ~22-entry microzap — nothing to keep in step across commits), each length checked; the layout is matched against
> every LAYOUTS entry (`zfs_wc_layout`); a missing attribute or layout is REFUSED by name. **Modes** (the ABI has no
> mode argument): files `0100644`, directories `040755`, symlinks `0120755` — FreeBSD's under umask 022, the
> fixtures' own (k.2); a directory inherits the parent's `S_ISGID`. **dn_flags** start 0 (USED_BYTES comes with a born
> block; the accounting bits from `zfs_wd_uacct`) — FreeBSD's empty files and symlinks carry `0xa`. **Times**: all four
> `{now, 0}` (`zfs_wf_now`: whole seconds; the op list's deterministic clock under ZFS_WRITE_OPS). **Quotas**: the space
> predicates for 512 B (`zfs_wd_space`) and the owner's user / group / project **byte and object** quotas
> (`zfs_acl_ids_overquota` — `zfs_wd_idq3`, whose object half is new with B6); a symlink's project is the default.
> **Refused** also: a parent with `ZFS_INHERIT_ACE` (inheritance not built), an xattr or immutable parent, a FUID group,
> a non-ASCII name on a `utf8only` dataset (UTF-8 validation not built), a symlink target over 144 bytes.
> **(B8, as built — `zfs_wc_layout`)** A layout the LAYOUTS ZAP does not hold is REGISTERED in the create's txg:
> number `zap_count + 2` (0 and 1 are the legacy layouts no ZAP entry names — `sa_add_layout_entry`), the decimal key,
> the attribute array as u16 integers (`zap_update`, through `zfs_wz_op` — LAYOUTS is always fat: its u16 arrays upgrade
> the microzap at the first add); a later create finds it through the reader like any other (the match reads T − 1's
> LAYOUTS). Refused by name: a normalised LAYOUTS ZAP, the 10-bit number space exhausted, a number already taken (a
> LAYOUTS ZAP that is not dense). On disk: `agnoswr`'s `rs4k` (a symlink in a child dataset), `agnostank/coff/kid` (the
> same), `agnosfz`'s `sym` (the root's first symlink) and `sym2` (its next txg: the layout `sym` registered, matched);
> `zfs-sa-check.py` holds the new numbers to `len(start layouts) + 2` onward, each array distinct, the start layouts
> unchanged; FreeBSD's `zdb -dddd` and the round trip read them.

### f.2 Directory entries

`value = obj | DT << 60`. Microzap add: name < 50 bytes, cd = lowest unused for that 28-bit hash, first free slot; grow
by 512 B up to 128 KiB, then upgrade. Upgrade on a ≥ 50-byte name, a full 128 KiB block, or (review fix) when the
new entry's hash already has too many same-hash entries to fit one fat leaf (`mze_canfit_fzap_leaf`, `zap_micro.c:1510-1519`): `fzap_upgrade` (16 KiB
blocks, `zap_salt = mz_salt`, each entry re-added with its own cd — with leaf splits as needed). Fat add/remove/split
per WPA § 9; external pointer-table entries updated on split; doubling an external table refused. Remove: micro zero
the entry; fat free chunks; empty leaves kept (merge is MAY). `zap_num_entries` exact.
> **(B6, as built)** The add is B3b's `zfs_mz_add` (first free chunk, lowest unused cd, + 512 B growth) after
> `zfs_wc_mzfit` judged it: a free chunk or growth room below 128 KiB, and fewer names sharing the hash than one fat leaf
> holds ((same + 1) × 5 chunks < 638) — every upgrade trigger REFUSES by name until B8 (a ≥ 50-byte name too). A new
> directory's microzap salt is derived from (objset, object, T) | 1 — odd, non-zero, and byte-reproducible for the op
> list's images (OpenZFS uses addresses). A fat-ZAP parent (`dn_maxblkid != 0`, or block 0 not `ZBT_MICRO`) is refused.
> **(B7, as built)** Remove = mzap_remove: the entry's 64-byte chunk zeroed (`zfs_wu_drop`, after checking it still holds
> the dirent the lookup returned); the microzap never shrinks. The parent's SA (SIZE − 1, LINKS − 1 for a directory,
> MTIME = CTIME = now, FLAGS | ARCHIVE | AV_MODIFIED) in place in its bonus (`zfs_wu_pstamp`); a fat-ZAP parent, a
> normalised one, an xattr / immutable / append-only one, or one keeping those attributes outside its bonus is refused
> by name.
> **(B8, as built — `zfs_wenc.cyr` § "B8: the FAT ZAP", `zfs_write.cyr` § "B8: ZAPs of every shape")** One ZAP path for
> every writer (directories, the user / group / project-used ZAPs, SA LAYOUTS, the DELETE_QUEUE, the MOS log ZAP):
> `zfs_wz_open` + `zfs_wz_get` / `zfs_wz_op` (add / update / remove), micro or fat by block 0. The fat code is OpenZFS
> 2.4.2's, function for function — `zap_leaf_init` / `chunk_alloc` / `chunk_free` / `array_create` / `array_copy` /
> `array_free` (2.4: the chain freed IN ORDER), `zap_entry_create` (`ZAP_NEED_CD`: a CDSORTED leaf's sorted chain, else
> each cd tried), `zap_entry_remove` (value, name, then the entry: "opposite order to reduce fragmentation"),
> `zap_entry_update`, `zap_leaf_transfer_entry`, `zap_leaf_split` (rehash in chunk order), `zap_expand_leaf` (the table
> grown first while the leaf's prefix is the table's), `zap_grow_ptrtbl` (embedded → one block, each entry twice) and
> `zap_table_grow` (one source block per call; `zap_table_store`'s dual writes while `zt_nextblk` is set; at the last
> copy the old blocks freed — dirtied ALL ZERO, the commit's hole of birth T), `zap_put_leaf_maybe_grow_ptrtbl`
> (`ZAP_LEAF_LOW_WATER` 20, or a doubling under way), `fzap_add_cd` / `fzap_update` / `fzap_remove` with
> `zap_num_entries`, `mzap_upgrade` + `fzap_upgrade` (block 0 resized to 16 KiB, the micro block snapshotted, the salt
> and normflags kept, every entry re-added in chunk order with its own cd). It runs over three resolvers (`zfs_zf_blk`
> dirtied — past `dn_maxblkid` the object grows, `zfs_wf_levels`; `zfs_zf_rblk` dirty-first; `zfs_zf_free`), so the
> HOST runs the same code (gate 37: two OpenZFS histories byte for byte, 13 cases — k.80). Every chunk index read from a
> block is checked before use (`zfs_zl_ok`; chains walked at most NUMCHUNKS steps) and an inconsistent block fails the
> op `fat ZAP inconsistent` with nothing written. Normalisation: `U8_TEXTPREP_TOUPPER` folds ASCII; a non-ASCII name in
> a normalised fat ZAP is refused (`u8_textprep` not built) — host-proven, unreachable on disk while normalised
> datasets are refused. **Upgrades** (`zfs_wz_op`): an add or update of a value that is not one u64, a name of 50+
> bytes, a hash `mze_canfit_fzap_leaf` refuses, or a full 128 KiB block (`large_microzap` is never activated: k.57).
> **Removals**: chunks freed, `lh_nentries` / `zap_num_entries` − 1, an emptied leaf KEPT (`zap_shrink` not built,
> k.76). The B6 / B7 refusals of a fat parent, a ≥ 50-byte name and a full microzap are gone; names run to 255 bytes.

### f.3 Recipes (each one txg; WPA § 10)

create/mkdir/symlink: alloc object + SA → `zap_add` → parent SIZE +1, LINKS +is_dir, MTIME/CTIME, FLAGS; new dir SIZE 2
LINKS 2. link: target LINKS +1, PARENT := new dir, CTIME. unlink: remove entry; LINKS −1; at 0 free the object (§ f.5).
rmdir: SIZE == 2 else refuse; remove; free. rename: same dataset; type match; walk PARENT from the target dir to the
root, refuse on meeting the source; target replaced (link_destroy); entry moved; PARENT := target dir; both dirs SIZE /
LINKS adjusted; `AV_MODIFIED`. write: data per WPA § 5 block-size rules (single-block growth to `min(recordsize,
roundup(end, 512))`; recordsize > 128 KiB used only where `large_blocks` is active on the dataset, else capped at
128 KiB); SIZE, MTIME, CTIME, FLAGS. `AO_TRUNC`: free range from 0, SIZE 0, clear `ZFS_SPARSE`.

### f.4 Writable fd and identity

New `VFS_ZFS_WFILE = 14` (`vfs.cyr:20-41`; payload pos / **GEN** / ref). Every fd operation re-reads the dnode and
compares `ZPL_GEN`; a mismatch (the object was unlinked and its number reused) fails −1 (D11 a — the NFS file-handle
model). Size comes from the SA on `read` and `SEEK_END`, not from a cached `payload[1]` (fixes the shared ext2 defect for
ZFS).
> **(B7, as built)** The check fires now (k.41, k.50 — on disk in the op list and in zfsx): `zfs_fd_size` answers -1 when
> the object's slot is free (unlinked and freed), its `ZPL_GEN` differs (the number reused by a create — first fit,
> measured: `agnos9`'s freed `b6/many/f03` went straight to the next create), or its `LINKS` is 0 (on the DELETE_QUEUE);
> an `AO_DIRECTORY` fd carries the GEN too (`zfs_open`) and `zfs_getdents` answers -1 after an `rmdir` or a reuse. Every
> `read`#5, `write`#1 and `SEEK_END` goes through it.
> **(B7 review)** The rule is ONE function, `zfs_fd_live` (the same GEN and, where GEN is kept, `LINKS` ≠ 0): the
> directory fd's `getdents` and `zfs_wtrunc`'s re-check between its txgs tested the GEN alone, so a directory an `rmdir`
> (or a rename over it) put on the DELETE_QUEUE still listed, and a truncation went on committing txgs against an object
> another CPU had lazily unlinked while `fs_lock` was released. On disk: `agnos9`'s `lzd`, `rmdir`'d onto the queue under
> an open `AO_DIRECTORY` fd (the knob below) — `getdents` -1.

### f.5 Unlink and the delete queue

Default: free the object in the unlink txg (all blocks, spill, slots; kill path § e.2). Files with `ZPL_XATTR ≠ 0`
(directory xattrs) or whose free would exceed the per-txg log bound go the **lazy** way instead: LINKS 0, CTIME, add
`"%llx"(obj) → obj` to `DELETE_QUEUE`; OpenZFS drains it at its next read-write mount (`zfs_dir.c:288-357`), zdb reports
"on delete queue", not leaked.
> **(B7, as built)** `zfs_wu_drop` → `zfs_wu_free` (the object's every BP, every level, children first, and its spill
> onto the B5 kill list flagged `ZWU_FREED` — no dnode to charge — then its 1 + `dn_extra_slots` slots zeroed; the user
> accounting falls out of `zfs_wd_uacct`'s diff: a zeroed new side) or, when the kill list or the extent list cannot
> hold it in this txg (`ZWF_FRMAX` 4,096 entries; `zfs_wf_xfit` — about 3,400 DVAs beside a removal's own blocks: k.10)
> or `ZPL_XATTR` is set, `zfs_wu_lazy` (`DELETE_QUEUE`, an UNLINKED_SET microzap of one block — else refused, B8;
> `LINKS` 0, nothing else stamped — `zfs_link_destroy`'s unlinked case). On disk: `agnostank/off/lazy.bin` (64 KiB of
> 4 KiB records) goes lazy under the op list's knob `zfs_wu_tight` (8 kill entries — `zfs_wc_tight`'s pattern); OpenZFS
> frees it at the round trip's writable mount, and the verify's B-acct subtracts the objects zdb lists "on delete
> queue" (A5) from its object identity. The REAL bound is probed in memory on a real object (`zfs_wt_lazyprobe`:
> `agnoswr/rs4k/r64k.bin` right after B5's 20 MiB of 4 KiB records — "too big for one txg", the txg dropped): a 16 MiB
> lazy file on disk was tried first and measured out — `wr`'s room after it cut B5's 20 MiB write short (and depends on
> which metaslabs the fixture build syncs, k.46), `main`'s list leaves no room at its end (k.64). FreeBSD itself frees every unlinked file through
> this queue (`zfs_remove_` → `zfs_unlinked_add`, freed at the vnode's last release), so the same-txg free is agnos's
> choice (WPA § 10: equally valid).
> **(B7 review)** Every lazy route now runs on disk, on `agnos9` (`zfs_wt_b7`): a rename's TARGET (`rs.txt` over the
> 8 KiB `lz2.bin`) and a DIRECTORY (`lzd`) under the knob — its meaning now "too big at its N-th kill entry", so 1 sends
> an empty directory (one microzap block) there — and the `ZPL_XATTR` route with no knob: `xd.txt`, whose xattr lives in
> an extended-attribute directory (fixture RECIPE 6: `xattr=dir` for it alone — no fixture object a B7 op could reach
> had one). zdb calls that object's xattr directory and the xattr in it "possibly leaked" until OpenZFS's drain — its
> path walk dies at the unlinked owner — so the verify's `ds_check` sets exactly those objects aside (k.72).
> **(B8)** `zfs_wu_lazy`'s queue add goes through `zfs_wz_op`: a DELETE_QUEUE of any shape — micro, upgraded at its
> 2,047th entry, or fat — takes it. No fixture holds a fat queue (FreeBSD's grows fat only past ≈ 2,000 queued
> objects); its add is the directory add's code on another object (k.85).
> **(B10, k.63 DECIDED — built, k.111)** "freed by later txgs" is agnos's own now: the op that queued an object drains
> it in the txgs after its own (`zfs_wu_end` → `zfs_wq_obj`, fs_lock released between them), one txg a step — whole
> when its free fits (`zfs_wu_free`; its queue entry removed through B8's ZAP path), else a bounded part of its data
> from the top first (B5's `zfs_wf_trunc1`: zfs_rmnode's `dmu_free_long_range`). An object holding an
> extended-attribute directory is refused by name and stays queued (zfs_rmnode's purge is not built); a mount drains
> nothing (G1). On disk: agnos9's `lz2.bin` / `lzd` (the B7 entries) freed and `xd.txt` refused; agnostank's
> `off/lazy.bin` in bounded parts; `off/b10.bin` through the production trigger.

## (g) Memory and code-size budget

**Memory.** The reader's 6 MiB area is unchanged (≈ 1.3 MiB tail free, `ZR_END` 0x4B1000). The writer gets a **second,
lazy `pmm_alloc_2mb_run(4)` = 8 MiB at rw mount** (direct map; failure → read-only with a named line):

| Range | Use |
|---|---|
| 0 – 4 MiB | dirty data blocks (the 4 MiB commit threshold) |
| 4 – 6 MiB | dirty metadata blocks (16 KiB / 128 KiB) + 128 KiB compression output |
| 6 – 7 MiB | metaslab bitmaps (allocated + deferred); resident count = 1 MiB / (2 × bitmap) ≥ 1 (review fix: "≤ 4 resident" fits only ≤ 4 GiB metaslabs at ashift 12; OpenZFS uses 4–16 GiB metaslabs on ≥ 1 TB vdevs, § c.2) |
| 7 – 7.5 MiB | log encode buffer (≤ 4 × 128 KiB) |
| 7.5 – 8 MiB | dirty index, txg ALLOC/FREE lists (≈ 8 k entries), DSL / user-accounting delta tables, per-metaslab `sm_obj`/`U`/`smp_alloc` arrays |
| **(B1, as built)** `0x780000` 64 KiB | mount-time dnode slots (`ZWA_DN`) |
| **(B1)** `0x790000` 128 KiB | per-metaslab table, `ZWK_MAXMS` = 2048 × 64 B (one metaslab per 16 GiB above 3 TB → a 32 TiB vdev) |
| **(B1)** `0x7B0000` 16 KiB / `0x7B4000` 16 KiB | log table (`ZWK_MAXLOG` 1024) / resident map |
| **(B1)** `[0x7B8000, 0x800000)` 288 KiB | free for B3's dirty index, ALLOC/FREE lists and delta tables |
| **(B2)** `0x7B8000` 8 KiB | the uberblock slot being committed (`ZWA_UBS`) |
| **(B3a)** `0x5E0000` 128 KiB | compression output (`ZWA_CMP`): the metadata arena is `[0x400000, 0x5E0000)` |
| **(B3a)** `0x7BA000` 64 KiB / `0x7CA000` 4 KiB / `0x7CB000` 4 KiB | dirty index 1,024 × 64 B / its hash 1,024 × u32 / the walk order 1,024 × u32 |
| **(B3a)** `0x7CC000` 64 KiB / `0x7DC000` 16 KiB | the txg's extents 4,096 × 16 B (ALLOC/FREE) / per-metaslab first-fit cursor 2,048 × 8 B |
| **(B3a review)** `0x7E0000` 32 KiB | the metaslab loader's own two dnode slots (`ZWA_LDN`, `zfs_wldn`): a load runs inside any allocation or free, so it no longer overwrites the `zfs_wdn` slots a caller may hold |
| **(B3a review)** `[0x7E8000, 0x800000)` 96 KiB | free for B4's DSL / user-accounting delta tables (was 128 KiB) |
| **(B3b)** — | no new area: log T is encoded straight into its dirty block, so `ZWA_LOGB` (512 KiB at `0x700000`) is UNUSED — free for B10's metaslab-SM appends; the op list's read-back saves the metaslab and log tables at `ZWA_OS + 0x300000` / `+ 0x320000` (the data arena, empty between commits; `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` only). Module `.bss`: the new rootbp (128 B), the U preset (2,048 bits = 256 B), a few scalars |
| **(B4)** `0x700000` 64 KiB / `0x710000` 448 KiB | `ZWA_LOGB` taken: the extent list SORTED for log T's encoding (`ZWA_XLS` — a copy, so a chain re-plan still undoes the list by position) / the MOS chain blocks' snapshot for a re-plan (`ZWA_CSN`, k.25). B10's metaslab-SM appends need their own room now |
| **(B4)** `0x7E8000` 4 KiB / `0x7E9000` 32 KiB / `0x7F1000` 4 KiB | the open txg's datasets, 32 × 128 B (`ZWA_DSX`, `zfs_wds_open`'s layout) / user-group-project deltas, 1,024 × 32 B (`ZWA_UQ`) / chain reservations a re-plan raised, 256 × 16 B (`ZWA_RSV`); `[0x7F2000, 0x800000)` 56 KiB free. Module `.bss`: the snapshot's rootbp (128 B), nine scalars |
| **(B8)** — | no new area: `mzap_upgrade`'s snapshot of the microzap (≤ 128 KiB) is `ZWA_CMP` (free outside a block's build); the B7 operand names grow to 2 × 256 B and the fat-ZAP normalised-name buffer is 264 B (module `.bss`); the op list alone (`ZFS_WRITE_OPS`) borrows `[0x7F2000, +16 KiB)` for a leaf's names (`zfs_wt_small_leaf`) |
| **(B9 / the merge)** `0x7F2000` 4,224 B / `0x7F3200` 32 KiB / `0x7FB200` 16 KiB | `ZWA_DSB` (the snapshot side) / `ZWA_DLE` (the deadlist entries) / `ZWA_TNL` (the op list's names, k.107 (f)); `[0x7FF200, 0x800000)` 3.5 KiB free |
| **(B10)** the area grows **8 → 10 MiB** (`ZWK_WRUNS` 5 runs of 2 MiB, k.114): `0x800000` 256 KiB / `0x840000` 512 KiB | `ZWA_GHO`, 1,024 ghost logs × 256 B (a destroyed log's deferral, k.108) / `ZWA_FSB`, a flushed metaslab's space map ALONE as a bitmap (≤ `ZWK_BMCAP`; `zfs_wl_flush`); `[0x8C0000, 0xA00000)` 1.25 MiB free. The flush's appended blocks, the U block and the destroyed logs' dnode blocks are ordinary dirty MOS blocks of the metadata arena (a flush takes what the admissions left — corrected by the B10 review's row below, k.119). The selftest's two stages use the data arena's scratch (`ZWA_OS + 0x240000` / `+ 0x2C0000`) and `ZWA_CMP` for gang headers (k.27: `ZWA_CMP + depth × 64 KiB`, free outside a block's build). Checked for overlap against every row above: none |
| **(B10 review)** the metadata arena grows **1.875 → 2.25 MiB** (`ZWK_MARENA` `0x240000`, `[0x400000, 0x640000)`); `ZWA_CMP` **`0x5E0000` → `0x640000`** (128 KiB); `ZWA_BM` **`0x600000` → `0x8C0000`** (1 MiB) | the arena's last 384 KiB is HYGIENE'S (`ZWL_HAR`: no admission reaches it, `zfs_wf_alim` — every admission's limit is the pre-review 1.375 MiB; the commit's plan sees 1.75 MiB beside `ZWF_MRSV`), k.119. The dry flush (`zfs_wl_hres`) uses `ZWA_FSB`; `zfs-wb3: gang`'s header copies `ZWA_CMP`. Free: `[0x660000, 0x700000)` 640 KiB (the bitmaps' old home), `[0x9C0000, 0xA00000)` 256 KiB, `[0x7FF200, 0x800000)` 3.5 KiB. Checked for overlap against every row above: none — ⚠ B11's track was told `[0x8C0000, END)` was free (k.114): the merge re-checks |
| **(B11)** — | **no new area** (checked against every row above: B11 adds no buffer to the writer area). Policy B keeps the pending txg in the existing dirty set; the deferral release walks the released log through the loader's own dnode slot (`zfs_wldn(1)`, free outside a load) and clears the resident metaslabs' deferred bitmaps in place (`ZWA_BM`); the op list's B11 ops read through the reader's `ZR_WBUF` (64 KiB). Module `.bss`: ≈ 20 scalars (`zfs_wp_*`, `zfs_wdef_*`, `zfs_wc_hold` / `_imax` / `_iwin`, `zfs_wr_cls`; `kirqw_*` in io.cyr) and, `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` only, the cut knob's 6-word map |
| **(the B10 / B11 merge, k.133)** — | **no new area** (re-checked against every row above after B10's review moved `ZWA_BM` to `[0x8C0000, 0x9C0000)` — the range B10's k.114 had called free: B11 adds no buffer anywhere in the writer area, and its op list reads through the reader's `ZR_WBUF`). `ZWA_GHO` keeps its 256 KiB but holds ≤ 3 ghosts now (k.133 (a): retired by `zfs_wdef_rel`); the release walks a ghost through `zfs_wldn(1)` like a log. Module `.bss`: three scalars (`zfs_wl_gmk`, `zfs_wl_gret`, `zfs_wt_flall`) |

**Image.** Gate 34: LOAD end ≤ `0x3D0000`; 1.57.11 leaves **262,728 B** (`state.md`), and region 1 has no room above
the RSP0 window — the next wall move needs a new home for the BSP stacks (CLAUDE.md). Measured reader density ≈ 39 B per
production line (98,752 B / ≈ 2,520 lines). Per-bite estimates are in § h; **total ≈ 4,950 lines ≈ 193 KB (± 25 % →
145–240 KB)**, leaving ≈ 70 KB at the estimate and **breaching the wall at the top of the range**. `ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST`
adds ≈ 350 lines ≈ 14 KB to its flag build. Every flag build must also fit (`build.sh` refuses a failing one), so the
largest existing flag build's headroom is measured before B1 (§ k). **Measured after B1 (2026-10-10):** B1 cost **41,328 B** (≈ 983 code lines at ≈ 42 B/line) against the 23 KB
estimate (+77 %); TEST+KTEST headroom 161,896 B < the remaining B2–B11 estimate ≈ 170 KB → per D12 the room is being
made now. **(B2 review, 2026-10-10 — all 62 flag builds the sweep and its smokes make, measured):** TEST+KTEST is NOT
the binding build — **`RING3_SELFTEST=1` is, at 127,960 B** after the B2 review fixes (then `EDGE_ABI_SELFTEST` 145,872;
TEST+KTEST 156,064; `TEST` 160,928; plain 209,824; the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 200,944). The measurements above covered
four builds; the room D12 makes must be sized against RING3_SELFTEST. `CYRIUS_DCE` is NOT available (cycc 6.6.6 compaction moves `.bss`/`.rodata` without re-pointing data
immediates; 6.6.20 declines compaction on kernels — filed: `cyrius/docs/development/issues/2026-10-10-agnos-kernel-dce-compaction-moves-data.md`),
so the BSP stacks are moving out of region 1. **Measured 2026-10-09** (the tree with kashi 1.1.0, +5,744 B): plain **256,984 B**;
`TEST=1 KTEST=1` **203,224 B — the binding build** (≈ 10 KB left at the 193 KB estimate, ≈ 37 KB over at +25 %);
`ZFS_SELFTEST` 253,184 B. `CYRIUS_DCE=1` would remove 203 unreachable functions (162,672 B; plain headroom → 419,656 B),
but that kernel **dies before its banner** under gnoboot + OVMF (`agnsh-smoke` attempt 3, after two firmware VOIDs) —
DCE is not a free lever until that death is understood. Savings already taken: no lz4/gzip compressor
(ZLE ≈ 40 lines), bitmaps instead of range trees, gang/dedup/clone kills by deferral (no gang walk, no DDT/BRT),
no spill creation, no ACL inheritance, no label/XDR writer. Cut order if a bite runs > 15 % over (D12): B10 →
fat-ZAP split (B8 reduced to in-leaf add/remove + upgrade) → B9 → link/symlink.

**ROOM (2026-10-12) — the image grew its room; the writer was not cut** (operator standing rule, grow before refactor,
2026-10-11 — § j). Eleven module arrays first touched after the direct map is live — 300,064 B of `.bss` — left the
image for ONE 2 MB pmm carve-out reached through the direct map (`core/room.cyr` `room_init`, main.cyr; design and prior
art `docs/architecture/kernel-image-room.md`): `pcpu_spawn_elf_buf` 64 KB, `klug_buf` 64 KB (an 8 KB early ring stays in

## (h) Bites

Every bite: `sh scripts/build.sh` + its flag build + gate 34; **G1 holds throughout — a read-only mount writes not one
byte** (h4 below). "Verify" = the guest oracle of § i on the bite's lanes. Lines are production lines; bytes at 39 B/line.

| # | Bite | Lines ≈ bytes | Gate (boot after each) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **B0** | **Oracle first** — `zfs-fixture.sh --verify DIR` + guest `verify_lane` (phases A–D) + host image audit (h1–h5) + pristine accounting baselines (RECIPE 2: new `wr` image without root snapshots + snapshot/readonly/formD/utf8only/quota/recordsize/dnodesize children, a near-full `full` pool; **RECIPE 3** (review): + clone / bookmark / casesensitivity=insensitive / zvol / copies=2 children, exact `$MOS` and `freeing`/`leaked` baselines, the cache keyed on the guest script's build half) | 0 kernel (≈ 750 script lines) | every pristine lane **PASSES**; deliberately damaged copies **FAIL in the named phase** (flip a data byte → A3's `-cc` rc 3 first, then C scrub (review fix: A runs before C, so A3 is the phase that goes red); bump an `smp_alloc` → "block traversal size" + `-mmmm` "INCONSISTENT" (measured: zdb's live ASSERT aborts first, k.5); drop a log ZAP entry → import ENOENT — only if some `U[i]` names that txg, so pick the entry by reading `U[]` first). **(B0 review)** + bad ditto copies only (MOS root DVA[1..2], an L1's DVA[1], a copies=2 L0's DVA[1]) → A3's `-s` vdev error table; `ds_referenced` +4096 → A5 + B-acct; root HEAD→CHILD → B-acct; a block charged to the wrong sibling → A5 + D-destroy; a lost final uberblock → H + A2 |
| **B1** | **Write gate + rw state load, no writes**: pool/dataset gates with named lines; MOS config nvlist (`vdev_zap_top`); `U[]`, log ZAP, metaslab array, SM + log replay into bitmaps with strict checks; ZIL classification; feature refcounts | 600 ≈ 23 KB | image hash identical after boot (G1); per-metaslab allocated bytes printed == `zdb -mmm` on the same image; gate lines as expected per lane (main rw with its per-dataset list, v28/mirror/raidz/makefs refused). **(review fix)** On a freshly *exported* fixture this barely exercises log replay — export flushes every metaslab (`spa.c:2060-2094`; B3b review: every metaslab that HAS a U — § c.3 — and none at all under `zfs_keep_log_spacemaps_at_export`), leaving only the last few txgs' logs — so the replay path is first really tested at B3's read-back boot over agnos's own logs; B1 must also print per-log entry counts and the number of metaslabs with unflushed entries, and the B3 gate repeats the zdb comparison after ≥ 20 agnos txgs |
| **B2** | `zfs_wr`, `zfs_eck_set`, flush ordering, **uberblock-only commit** (ub T0+1 with the same `ub_rootbp`) under `ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST` only | 150 ≈ 6 KB | h1–h3 (only slot `(T0+1) % N` changed, in all 4 labels, valid eck); `zdb -uuu` txg = T0+1; import + `zdb -bcc` + scrub clean. If OpenZFS rejects a ub whose rootbp is older than its txg (§ k.1), drop B2 into B3. **BUILT 2026-10-10** — the trigger is its own flag, `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` (the op list, `zfs_wtest`; `ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST` alone still writes nothing, G1); `scripts/smoke/zfs-write-smoke.sh` PASS: ashift9 / wr / main commit T0+1 (24 / 77 / 99), audit h1–h5 + `--same-rootbp`, guest verify 30/30 steps per lane; v28 + mirror-a refused, h4 identical. ≈ 260 lines (≈ 180 code), plain image +3,888 B. **(B2 review, 2026-10-10: nine minor findings)**: the gate scores a native ring slot only when its eck verifies (OpenZFS's rule — a torn T0+1 had locked the pool read-only for agnos for good) and refuses T0 copies with bytes past 216; a USB stick without SYNCHRONIZE CACHE answers its flush 0 (`msc_blk_flush`, was -1 against its own comment); `blk_readonly_on` + the gate's `device is write-protected`; the label gate requires each label's pool_guid + leaf guid; the deterministic clock is `max(…, previous)` and audit h3 checks timestamps; the op list runs on `agnos*` fixture pools only; QEMU_DWELL_VOID in both ZFS smokes; the smoke grows to 12 lanes (+ torn / grown-partition / AHCI / USB variants byte-identical to the NVMe lane, a read-only drive through `zfs_wfail`, makefs refused, raidz-0 no pool); commit hold times recorded in § k.8. Review fixes ≈ 56 code lines, plain image +1,944 B (headroom 209,824 B) |
| **B3** | **The null txg**: dirty-buffer engine, BP builder (fletcher4/sha256/sha512 dispatch, ZLE compressor, holes, fills), MOS object alloc, log SM create + encode, log-ZAP micro add **with 512 B growth** (review fix: microzaps never shrink, but a log ZAP that never held more than 7 logs is still 512 B = 7 entries, and it grows by 512 B per full add, `zap_micro.c:675-690`; without growth the 8th unflushed agnos txg cannot commit), in-place ZAP value update, `U[i]`, ditto allocation, `$MOS`/root accounting, chain planning, commit + error path | 1,100 ≈ 43 KB | full verify: A1–A4 (incl. `-t T−1`, `-t T−2`), `zdb -y`, import, scrub, export → `zdb -bcc` again, OpenZFS round trip on top; host unit vectors (ZLE round trip, SM entry/BP encode) in `host-zfs-oracles.sh`. **B3a BUILT 2026-10-10** (the host-testable half, no on-disk commit): `core/zfs_wenc.cyr` (new, pure: BP words, holes, the ZLE decision, fills, space-map entries) + `zfs_codec.cyr` `zfs_zle_compress` / `zfs_cksum_words` (the reader's `zfs_ck_ok` now goes through it) + `zfs_write.cyr` dirty set, walk order, txg extent list, allocator (slop, first fit, `metaslab_is_unique` ditto rule), `zfs_wfree_bp`, `zfs_bp_build`; § c.2's pin rule replaced by reconstruction (note there). ≈ 614 production code lines: plain image **−26,704 B** (headroom 311,056), TEST+KTEST 255,104, **RING3_SELFTEST 229,200 (binding)**, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 291,912 (+≈ 10 KB of selftest). Gate 37: 605 cases (floor 605, was 249), incl. 14 block pointers, 5 physical blocks, 3 dnode blocks and 4 space maps OpenZFS wrote — a real ZLE data block's psize **and** bytes reproduced by `zfs_wcomp`; 43 mutants, 42 red, 1 equivalent. `ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST` adds `zfs_wst_b3`: the same code in memory on each rw pool's real bitmaps, every BP judged by the reader's checksum/decompress code, all undone (`zfs-wb3: done ok`, required by both ZFS smokes; G1 still byte-identical). check.sh 37/37; zfs-smoke 7/7; zfs-write-smoke 12/12 + OpenZFS verify 3/3 (8 min 18 s). Open questions for B3b: § k.17–k.21. **B3a REVIEW (2026-10-10, 9 findings, all fixed):** the no-pin rule now RUNS — `zfs_wst_b3`'s evict stage holds the residency at one slot on every rw lane (eviction of a touched metaslab, `zfs_wxl_apply` with ALLOC + FREE extents, a 3-copy allocation evicting as it goes, `zfs_wxl_undo` on non-resident metaslabs; byte snapshots + pre-exercise digests of every metaslab); FREE accounting where the two bitmaps overlap (§ c.2 note: newly-deferred count, `zfs_xl_pre`, evict-on-undo); `zfs_wfree_bp` refuses dedup and crypt-bit BPs; DMU_OT_IS_VALID in both BP encoders; the loader's own dnode slots (`ZWA_LDN`, § g) and `zfs_wscr` on every mount-scratch writer (k.23); a failed exercise returns everything and leaves the pool read-only, and the op list refuses; word 6 of every built BP checked field by field. Gate 37: **650 cases (floor 651 with the local one)** — the ZLE floor's exact edge (output == / == + 1 of s_len − s_len/8) and exact 2×/3× allocation-unit outputs; 55 host mutants, **54 red, 1 equivalent** (the review's three survivors now red); 9 kernel mutants of `zfs_write.cyr` (apply / load / undo / free / BP build), each booted once on `ashift9`: **9 red**, and a failing exercise in the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build skips the op list with the pool byte-identical. Cost: production +2,184 B (plain headroom 308,872; TEST+KTEST 252,928; **RING3_SELFTEST 227,016**, binding); the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 279,104 (selftest +≈ 10.6 KB). B3b: k.22 (a lane whose metaslabs exceed the slots), the `full` lane for the overlap rule. **B3b BUILT 2026-10-10 — THE NULL TXG, end to end** (`zfs_write.cyr` § "B3b"; § c.2–c.4, § d notes): `zfs_commit` plans txg T in the writer area — log space map T (a new MOS object, every ALLOC and FREE of T including its own block's and every rewritten MOS block's), `spacemap_histogram` + 1, the log-ZAP add with 512 B growth, U[] in rounds (§ c.3), the MOS's space up the dsl_dirs, dn_used, fills — finalises non-chain blocks (ZLE), allocates the chain (OFF), encodes log T, fills the chain bottom-up, then B2's uberblock with the new rootbp (`zfs_commit_ubw`) between two flushes, then publishes (`zfs_wc_pub`: the mount's own scan sums log T). `zfs_wr` opens to extents allocated in T. Failure before the uberblock = `zfs_wfail_now` (named), no reload needed (copy-on-write until durable). Write gate + `zfs_wgate_objs` (the objects every commit changes, in the shape it writes). Op list: ub-only + **3 null txgs** (8 on `full`), each followed by `zfs_wt_rb` — the next mount's whole state load re-run from the device and compared with what was published. **zfs-write-smoke 14/14, OpenZFS verify 5/5** (ashift9, wr, wr@u0, main, full: A1–A6 with A4's rewinds onto agnos's own null txgs, `zdb -y`, `-mmmm`, import, scrub, OpenZFS writes on top, export → zdb, D freeing/leaked 0), the four variants byte-identical to ashift9, the refused lanes byte-identical; 13 min 16 s. New lanes: `full` (one bitmap slot, metadata into the slop, eight txgs — the log ZAP outgrows 512 B: k.17, k.21, k.22) and `wr@u0` (`zfs-damage.py zero-u`, the upgraded-pool state: two rounds, U = T on disk — § c.3); `ZWS_LANES` partial runs for the negative proofs; the audit's `--same-rootbp T` and the verify's `<lane>.pristine`. Negative proofs (scratch mutants, the in-kernel read-back silenced so only the guest judges): see § i.4. MOS shape and costs: k.3, k.8, k.19–k.21; B4's questions: k.24–k.29. **Cost:** ≈ 625 production code lines (598 in the B3b section; ≈ 41 B per line): plain image **−25,760 B** (headroom 283,112), TEST+KTEST 227,184, **RING3_SELFTEST 201,272 (binding)**, `ZFS_SELFTEST`+`ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST` 256,080, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 248,880 (the op list and read-back +≈ 4.5 KB). ~~B3 as a whole (B3a + B3b) ≈ 1,240 lines ≈ 52.5 KB against the 43 KB estimate: +22 %~~ **B3 as a whole (B3a + the B3a review + B3b) = 54,648 B of the plain image (headroom 337,760 → 283,112) against ≈ 42,900 B: +27 %** (B3b review: the 52.5 KB left out the B3a review's 2,184 B) — over the § g 15 % mark. The wall: 201 KB left on the binding build against ≈ 123 KB of B4–B11 estimates — ≈ 156 KB at B3's +27 %, inside it; ≈ 218 KB at B1's +77 %, OVER it. Per D12 a report, not a stop; re-weigh after B4 with the +27 % ratio (§ g). **B3b REVIEW (2026-10-10, 7 findings — 1 major, 6 minor; all confirmed and fixed):** (major) the premise "one round on any pool OpenZFS exported" was false for every upgraded pool — an export flushes only the metaslabs that have a U, so `U = 0` survives it; § c.3, k.3, k.24 and `zfs_commit`'s convergence comment corrected (the code was right: rounds converge); B3's cost restated (+27 %, above and § g); `ZWS_SPACE=advisory` gives the negative proofs their guest phase back (all four re-run: h6 now catches N1, N3, N4 first — § i.4); the overlap rule (§ c.2) now runs ON DISK — `zfs-damage.py pre-defer` on `wr@u0` (evict-on-undo: `rounds=2 loads=3 pre=3`) and on a new lane `full@pre` (re-apply at reload: `pre=3 reapply=24`), the plan line printing `pre=` / `reapply=`, OpenZFS 30/30 on both; four doc fixes (N3's crash risk is build-dependent and stays in the rationale; log T is 1 KiB at ashift 9; k.7 answered by `zfs_wcomp`; the log ZAP grows within its one block, 512 → 1,024 B); hold times re-measured (§ b.3, k.8) and the chain's no-gang ENOSPC exposure added to k.25; audit h3 now requires every null txg's rootbp to be born in its own txg and to differ from the previous one (§ i.2 — the old h3 passed a re-published MOS). Cost: production **+64 B** (the `zfs_xl_rap` counter): plain headroom 283,048, TEST+KTEST 227,104, **RING3_SELFTEST 201,192 (binding)**, `ZFS_SELFTEST`+`ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST` 256,016, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 248,632. Gates: zfs-write-smoke **15/15, OpenZFS verify 6/6** (14 min 55 s); zfs-smoke 7/7; zfs-ring3-smoke PASS; check.sh 37/37; aarch64 the same 32 undefined functions |

Plus k.17 (decided: metadata into the slop, user data checked twice), **k.24** (decided + built: U = T preset at the first commit, the round loop a named failure) and **k.25** (decided + built: reserved-size ZLE for the MOS chain indirects with snapshot / undo / re-plan, ≤ 3; a null txg 125,952 B at ashift 9 / 172,032 B at 12 instead of 514,560 / 552,960). **B4 gate record (2026-10-10).** Builds — every one through image-layout (headroom to the `0x400000` wall): plain
246,832 B · TEST+KTEST 186,184 · **RING3_SELFTEST 164,976 (binding)** · KTEST+THREAD+KSTACK 185,584 · the
`ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 208,584; aarch64 the same 32 undefined functions and 17 variables. **zfs-write-smoke PASS, 15/15
lanes, OpenZFS verify 6/6** (ashift9, wr, wr@u0, main, full, full@pre — 30 steps each: A1–A6 with A4's rewinds onto
agnos's null txgs, T − 1 the re-planned one; B with the B-acct identities, `referenced == usedbydataset` and the hidden
children on every head; C with OpenZFS appending to agnos's rewritten `rand-2m.bin` / `fill-0.bin`; D destroying every
child ONE AT A TIME — the COW'd `quota`, `resv`, `sha256`, `coff/kid` included — with `freeing` / `leaked` unchanged after
each and 0 at the end), the four variants byte-identical to ashift9, the wfail lane refusing its dataset COW, the
refused lanes byte-identical; per rw lane the host's `space` (h6) and `uacct` (h7) and, after the verify, `uspace`
(OpenZFS's `zfs userspace / groupspace / projectspace` == the recount: 3 / 39 / 39 / 54 / 3 / 3 lines); 14 min 54 s
(the final run, with h7's dsl_dir check; the first full run before it, 15 min 2 s).
One ORACLE fix on the way (§ e.1's row): B-acct's allocated identity went red on `wr`'s `resv/r.txt` COW (28,672 B moved
allocated and not the root's used, through a refreservation and a reservation) with agnos's accounting exactly
OpenZFS's; it now subtracts the unused reservations. Host vectors (gate 37): 927 cases (+276), 16/16 mutants red.
**Negative proofs** (scratch mutants of `zfs_write.cyr` applied with cyim, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` kernel built, one
lane, `ZWS_SPACE=advisory` so the image reaches the guest, `ZWS_CONTINUE=1` so the guest runs every phase; the source
restored byte-identical after each): **N1** `ds_referenced` + one allocation unit → `uacct` red ("off by 512"), guest
**H, A5, B-acct** (`referenced != usedbydataset`), C-k15, D-root, D-zdb red; **N3** the group deltas skipped →
`uacct` red (`groupused '0'` 2,115,584 vs the recount 2,116,608), guest **H** red (the only guest phase that reads
those bytes — the rest of B-acct checks the uid-0 object count) and `uspace` red (OpenZFS's own groupspace disagrees);
**N4** a data block's predecessor not killed → `uacct` red (`dn_used` 2,232,320 != Σ dsize 2,101,248), guest **H, A3,
A4** red (zdb "leaked space: vdev 0, offset 0x18000, size 131072"); **N2** the dataset dir's parents not moved
(`zfs_wdd_use` stopped at the dataset's own dir) — ⚠ on `wr` the first run was **GREEN in every phase**: neither of
its COWs moves USED bytes past the dataset's dir (`q.bin`'s rewrite has equal dsize, `r.txt`'s is absorbed by the
refreservation and the reservation), so only the parents' compressed / uncompressed went stale, and no oracle checked
them. Closed in h7: every snapshot-free dsl_dir's compressed / uncompressed == its head's + its child dirs' (exact on
every pristine image). Re-run on `wr` and `main`: `uacct` red on both (`agnoswr` off by −2,048 / −1,536;
`agnostank/coff` the same), guest **H** red on both, and on `main` (where `coff/kid`'s 28,672 B must reach `coff` and the
root) **B-acct** (`coff` usedbychildren 98,304 vs Σ children 126,976; Δ allocated vs the root's used), **D-destroy**
(destroying `coff` left `freeing` at −28,672: 18,446,744,073,709,522,944) and **D-free** red. Commit hold (QEMU/KVM, the kernel's timestamps):
dataset COW txgs 0.62–0.76 s, null txgs 0.59–0.71 s (AHCI / usb-storage 0.62–0.69 — B3b's 0.73–0.87: k.25 cut the
write volume), ub-only 0.20–0.84 s. **zfs-smoke PASS 7/7** (G1: every read-only lane byte-identical; one firmware
VOID retried); **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS** (the plain kernel; zfsx exit 95); **check.sh 37/37** (gate 37: 927 cases).

**B4 REVIEW (2026-10-10, 5 findings — 1 major, 4 minor; all confirmed, all fixed).** (major) **A wrong refreservation
split was invisible until phase D destroyed that dataset** — and never for a root dataset, which D does not destroy:
B-acct's allocated identity subtracts Σ `usedbyrefreservation`, identities 1 / 2 sum REFRSRV back into used, and h7
read only compressed / uncompressed. Reproduced (the review's N5: `zfs_wd_dsdelta`'s `parent_delta` replaced by the
whole dsize — `resv`'s dir +28,672 B used, REFRSRV untouched): green through uacct, H, A, B, C, red only at D. Fixed on
both sides (§ e.1, § i.2, § i.3): h7 now reads every dsl_dir's breakdown from the MOS (Σ == used, CHILD /
CHILD_RSRV from the child dirs, REFRSRV == max(0, refreservation − `ds_unique_bytes`), a non-clone head's HEAD ==
referenced) and recounts every head's `ds_unique_bytes` from its blocks (== referenced when snapshot-free and no clone,
else Σ dsize born after `ds_prev_snap_txg`) — exact on 38 pristine dirs, snapshotted / clone / zvol / encrypted heads
among them; and the guest's B-acct gains identity 6, `usedbyrefreservation == max(0, refreservation − written)`, with
`written` OpenZFS's deadlist-derived unique bytes — exact on every pristine `.acct` and B4 capture (52 heads) before it
was wired. (minor) **Policy refusals at the commit**: the accounting ZAP blocks are first dirtied inside the commit, so
a gang / dedup / snapshot-owned ZAP block, a full microzap or an overflowing delta table failed the pool read-only;
now judged at dirty time (§ e.3 note: `zfs_wds_open` runs `zfs_wd_okbp` on every accounting ZAP BP, refuses one with
indirects, records the new-key capacity; `zfs_wd_dirty1` reserves 6 table entries + 2 keys per ZAP where T − 1's dnode
lacks the flags); what can still fail inside a commit is named in § k.37 and `zfs.md` § 10. (minor)
**Coverage**: the first dataset COW of every pool now re-plans its MOS chain on disk (`zfs_wc_tight`; `replans=1`
required on its plan line, `loads=74` on `full` at one slot); the reservation crossings and the accounting-key add /
remove-at-0 / growth paths need a fixture RECIPE 4, which a fixture build cannot make here without sweeping a stale work
dir this review was told to keep (§ k.37), so they run IN MEMORY at every rw mount — `zfs-wb3: uacct … 512->1024 B,
reversed to T-1 ok` and `zfs-wb3: resv … crossing / absorbed / above, each there and back ok`, required by both ZFS
smokes. (minor) **Deferred frees in the space predicates** (k.17 note): `zfs_wroom` = normal class − slop −
`zfs_w_defer`, the txg's frees kept busy; `zfs_w_defer` equals the deferred bitmaps' popcount on every lane (`wr`:
221,184 B at mount → 1,212,416 after six txgs), the read-back re-derives it, the plan line prints `defer=`; `full`'s COW
still fits (2,043,904 − 233,472 B deferred at mount). (minor) **Stale comments**: the B3b commit header removed, the
ZWD flags' "bits 16-31" clause replaced by where the k.25 reservation really lives.
**Gates (re-run in full):** builds — plain **245,432 B** · TEST+KTEST 184,784 · **RING3_SELFTEST 163,592 (binding)** ·
KTEST+THREAD+KSTACK 184,200 · ZFS_SELFTEST+ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST 201,152 · the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 189,680; aarch64 the
same 32 undefined functions and 17 variables. **zfs-write-smoke PASS 15/15, OpenZFS verify 6/6** (30 steps each, B-acct
with identity 6 on every head, D freeing / leaked 0), 13 min 40 s; **zfs-smoke PASS 7/7** (the new `b3a` lines on both rw
lanes, 1 min 44 s); **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS** (the plain kernel); **check.sh 37/37**. Commit hold: dataset COW txgs 0.60–0.70 s (the re-planned ones
included), null txgs 0.59–0.64 s.
**Negatives re-run** (scratch mutants via cyim, the source restored byte-identical after each). Plain — the mutation
everywhere: **N1** (`ds_referenced` + one unit), **N2** (no parent propagation), **N3** (group deltas skipped), **N5**
(no dataset-level `parent_delta`) are now caught AT MOUNT by the in-memory stages (`zfs-wb3: FAIL resv: …` /
`FAIL uacct: …`; the pool read-only, the op list skipped, nothing written). Gated on the op list (`zfs_wt_n > 0`, so
only the commits are mutated — the oracle proof, `ZWS_SPACE=advisory ZWS_CONTINUE=1`): **N5g** (`wr`) → `uacct` red
(`agnoswr/resv: REFRSRV 8290304 != max(0, refreservation 8388608 - ds_unique_bytes 126976) = 8261632`), guest **H,
B-acct** (identity 6) red BEFORE phase D, then D-destroy / D-free; **N1g** (`ashift9`) → `uacct` (unique / HEAD != referenced),
**H, A5, B-acct, C-k15, D-root, D-zdb**; **N2g** (`wr`, `main`) → `uacct` both (`agnostank/coff: CHILD 98304 != Σ the
child dirs' dd_used 126976`), **H** both, `main` **B-acct, D-destroy, D-free**; **N3g** (`ashift9`) → `uacct`, **H**,
`uspace`; **N4** (`ashift9`) → `uacct`, **H, A3, A4, B-acct, C-zdb, D-zdb**.
**Cost of the fixes:** production +1,400 B (≈ 45 lines: `zfs_wroom` and `zfs_w_defer`, the dirty-time accounting
checks); the in-memory stages +≈ 17.5 KB in the `ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST` builds only. B4 as a whole: 32,352 B of the
plain image against § g's 17,550 B (**+84 %**); the re-weigh is in § g.

**zfs-write-smoke PASS 15/15, OpenZFS verify 6/6** (23 min 32 s; the one guest boot verifying six lanes, 636 s): every rw lane's file-data ops as § i.1 lists them,
each txg one round with its read-back, every written file's last F line and the walk after the list == the expected
manifest (`zfs-expect.py` + the regenerated originals), host h6 `space` / h7 `uacct` / h8 `holes`, the guest's 30 steps

zero-u` found no metaslab to zero and `wr@u0` failed its FORGE (no boot) — the forge had depended on build luck; it now

on the forged start) and the lane passes as before (`pre=3`, one round, OpenZFS 30/30). **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS**: zfsx
exit 95 with clauses 100–113 (five txgs on `agnostank/custom`), host h1–h3 + h6 / h7 / h8, OpenZFS 30/30 on
`main@ring3` (T0 + 5 = 103). **zfs-smoke PASS 7/7** (1 min 50 s; G1 — every read-only lane byte-identical — on the re-keyed fixture). **check.sh 38/38** (50 s; gate 37: 958 cases, the floor raised from 927).
**Commit hold** (QEMU/KVM, the kernel's timestamps, an op's begin → its commit line — one txg each; the final run): ashift9
NVMe file-data txgs 616–635 ms (-torn 625–645, -grown 614–627), AHCI 654–768, usb-storage 663–789; wr 620–644, wr@u0
621–635; main 630–699 (the RMW of a 1 MiB `big1m` block and its truncation the longest); `full` / `full@pre` (one bitmap
slot) the 3-txg fill 1,531 / 1,570 ms for all three (≈ 450–500 ms a txg: 2, 1, 1 blocks, 61–63 metaslab loads each), the
null txgs after it 621–701. The write path's own share is small: a file-data txg holds
`fs_lock` as long as a null txg (0.6–0.7 s) — the flushes and the MOS chain, not the data (k.8, k.25).
**Negative proofs** (scratch mutants of `zfs_write.cyr` via cyim, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` kernel built, the source restored
byte-identical — sha256 checked — after each; one lane, `ZWS_SPACE=advisory ZWS_CONTINUE=1` so the image reaches the
guest and every phase runs): **NA** SIZE written one short after an extend (`zfs_wf_write1`) on `ashift9` → the host's F
lines and the walk red (agnos's own reader: e00 12 vs 13, e01, hello.txt), guest **B-manifest** red (C-ops / C-k15 / C-rt
follow: OpenZFS reads the file short); phase A green — zdb does not check SIZE. **NB** every freed block's hole born 0
(both free paths) on `main` → **h8** red (`big1m` object 2 L0 2, `sha512` object 3 L0 0: "a hole of birth 0 where the
pristine image had a block"), guest **H only** (the audit's h8) — every OpenZFS phase green: the gap k.12 named, now
closed on the host. **NC** a truncation's kill skipped (`zfs_wd_phase`) on `ashift9` → `uacct` red (rand-2m.bin's
`dn_used` 2,097,152 vs Σ dsize 0), guest **H, A3, A4, B-acct, C-zdb, D-zdb** red (zdb's leaked space). **ND** an L1
fill + 1 in the dataset phase on `ashift9` (every L1 it finalised — the meta-dnode's among them) → every host check
green (none reads fills), guest **A5** red (zdb: "0: Block pointer fill (32) does not match calculated value (31)", and
SIGABRT on its object-count ASSERT: 31 object rows vs the header's 32 — the meta-dnode's L1 fill IS the object count)
and C-k15 (the same machinery on the round-tripped root). Each red where the oracle says.

**B5 REVIEW (2026-10-11, 9 findings — 4 major, 5 minor; all confirmed, all fixed).** Each major was a way for ring 3
to put the pool read-only until reboot through a failing COMMIT, or to hold `fs_lock` without bound. (1, major) **A
truncation's kill list was never measured against the extent list** (4,096 freed 4 KiB L0s of a 20 MiB file:
`txg extent list full: extents 4096`) — every listed kill now counts its DVAs and every dirty block 6 extents with
`ZWF_CRSV` (96) more (`zfs_wf_xfit`, shared by both free paths through `zfs_wf_kill`), `ZWF_FRMAX` = `ZWK_MAXXL`; k.28. (2,
major) **Log T was sized from the dirty-block count alone** (≈ 230 non-adjacent freed blocks overflowed a 4 KiB log) —
now from the extents already recorded + 6 per MOS block still to finalise, capped at `ZWK_MAXXL` (≤ 98,320 B: one
block at any metaslab count); k.28. (3, major) **The room was OpenZFS's figure over metaslabs agnos never allocates in**
(4 of 15 synced on `wr`: 212 one-block txgs later a commit found no metaslab) — `zfs_wroom` = min(OpenZFS's figure,
`zfs_wphys`), a txg opens only while `ZWK_MDRES` is left, OpenZFS's quota model on `dd_used` keeps its own figure
(`zfs_wroomz`: on the first attempt the min reached the dsl_dir walk too, and `wr`'s reservations cut its 20 MiB write
SHORT at 18.7 MB — caught by the lane, fixed before any gate); § a.3, k.40, k.46, k.47. (4, major) **One `write`#1 held
`fs_lock` across all its txgs** — released between them (`zfs_wf_yield`), the fd re-checked (tag, object, writable bit,
`ZPL_GEN`, the position it left) or the write ends SHORT, the truncation's object by `ZPL_GEN`; a truncating open
RESERVES its fd slot meanwhile; ABI § 3.6, k.8, k.48. (5) **A grown block 0 the write starts past, and a truncation's
boundary block, were user data no predicate charged** — charged now (`zfs_wd_space`; the boundary block by the
allocation rule alone, `zfs_wd_aroom` — a free may not be refused by a quota). (6) **User / group / project quotas** —
ENFORCED (`zfs_wd_idq`, before each txg as OpenZFS before each chunk), not refused; § a.3, k.45. (7) **Oracle gaps** —
host `zfs-sa-check.py` (every written file's MTIME / CTIME == the op list's clock, FLAGS ⊇ ARCHIVE | AV_MODIFIED, SIZE,
the rest of the SA untouched; COW-only files SA-identical), `zfs-room-check.py` (a short write's length against the
space record: UPPER — at most the room it started with, ADMIT — its last txg's block fit when admitted, LOWER — the
refused block did not), phase C's first op-touched file OUTSIDE the root dataset (`big1m/rand-3m.bin` on `main`,
`copies2/c512k.bin` on `wr`: overwritten mid-file and appended by OpenZFS, then the round trip), h8's LSIZE rule and its
birth rule (every writer-punched hole's birth is the txg of an op that freed or zeroed that file — with the serial log;
a first rule by written ranges was wrong: dbuf_handle_indirect_hole stamps every child of a re-materialised punched
indirect, so `wr`'s rs4k L1s 1018–1023 legitimately carry the truncation's birth). (8) **A multi-txg truncation could
refuse in a later txg** — every block it frees or rewrites is judged first (`zfs_wf_okscan`); ABI § 3.6 names what a
later failure still leaves; k.43. (9) **statfs's `f_bavail`** is the writer's room on an rw pool, OpenZFS's slop
otherwise (§ a.4). **New gates on disk:** `wr` — 20 MiB of 4 KiB records written THROUGH AN FD (11 txgs, the lock
released between them) then AO_TRUNC'd through `zfs_open` (5,137 kills in 2 txgs: 3,553 + 1,790 extents), then a burst of
one-block appends refused at its 82nd write by name (`no space: the metaslabs with a space map are full`, 331,776 B
landed); `main` — 250 one-block appends (250 txgs) and the truncation back that frees them in ONE txg (345 extents, a
4,736 B log); every plan line's `udata` ≤ `charged`. **Gates (full):** zfs-write-smoke **PASS 15/15, OpenZFS verify 6/6**
(30 steps each; 21 min 44 s, the guest 579 s; `main` 264 logs, `wr` 104); **zfs-smoke PASS 7/7** (G1 byte-identical,
1 min 57 s); **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS** (zfsx exit 95, h1–h3 + h6–h8, OpenZFS 30/30 on `main@ring3`); **check.sh 38/38**;
aarch64 unchanged. **Commit hold, re-measured per txg** (k.8: the op list's own ≈ 0.45 s plan-line print is no longer
inside it): ashift9 NVMe 201–230 ms, AHCI 234–347, usb-storage 247–409; `wr` 211–521 (mean 422 — the 2 MiB data txgs),
`main` 209–442, `full` 208–426. **Negative proofs** (scratch mutants via cyim, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` kernel built, the
source restored byte-identical — sha256 checked — after each; the lanes named, no guest needed: each went red on the
host): the old extent rule (no `zfs_wf_xfit`, `ZWF_FRMAX` 14,563) on `wr` → `commit txg 95 failed: txg extent list full:
extents 4096`; the old log bound on `main` → `commit txg 359 failed: log space map over its planned size`; no
`zfs_wphys` on `wr` → `commit txg 190 failed: no space: no metaslab holds bytes 16384` and room **ADMIT** red (−5.2 MB
of room before the last txg); no growth charge on `ashift9` → `udata` > `charged` red; CTIME stamped one second off →
`sa` red on `wr` and `full`; the fill stopping 1 MiB early on `full` → room **LOWER** red (872,448 B were left); a
punched hole's LSIZE + 512 → h8 red on `main` (`big1m`, `sha512`); births stamped on re-materialised birth-0 holes on
`wr` → h8 "extra birth" red. Not proven by a mutant: the lock release (single CPU in the op list; the fd path runs it
in `wr`'s list), the pre-scan's refusal (no fixture has a refusable block past a file's first txg), the quotas (no
fixture sets one, k.45). Open for B6+: k.44 (ruling), k.45–k.49.

**B6 gate record (2026-10-11) — CREATE / MKDIR / SYMLINK in microzap parents** (`zfs_write.cyr` § "B6"; § f.1 / f.2
"(B6, as built)", `zfs.md` § 12). `open`#7's `AO_CREAT` (a file, `0100644`; `AO_EXCL` before `AO_TRUNC`; never
through a final component that resolves; a new file not truncated — k.44 DECIDED and recorded), `mkdir`#9 (`040755`, a
512-byte microzap, SIZE 2, LINKS 2) and `symlink`#63 (`0120755`, the target in the bonus, ≤ 144 B) — one txg each through
the B4 dataset phase: the first free slot of the meta-dnode (`zfs_wc_slot`; a new dnode block past `dn_maxblkid` when
none is free), zfs_mknode's SA in the layout the dataset already holds (REGISTRY by name, LAYOUTS matched — k.2), the
trivial ACL and flags of the mode, GEN = T, the four times, PARENT, LINKS; the entry in the parent's microzap (B3b's
`zfs_mz_add` after `zfs_wc_mzfit`), its SIZE + 1, LINKS + 1 for a directory, MTIME / CTIME, FLAGS; the new object
accounted by the commit's own dnode diff (`zfs_wd_uacct`: a zero slot has no old side) with its reservations taken at
dirty time; the owner's byte AND object quotas (`zfs_wd_idq3`). Every § a.3 refusal named (three B6 rows added there).
**Fixture**: the payload change (zfsx clauses 114–126) re-keyed it, `r4-1839cf8d4036` → **`r4-75e62fdc8e42-a2e7083d`**
(the recipe unchanged: every dataset B6 creates in already holds a FreeBSD-made object of each kind it makes — the twins
`zfs-sa-check.py` and B-acl compare with; k.2's facts re-measured identical on the rebuilt images).
**Builds** (image-layout headroom to the `0x400000` wall): plain **191,072 B** · TEST+KTEST 130,424 · **RING3_SELFTEST
109,216 (binding)** · KTEST+THREAD+KSTACK 129,824 · ZFS_SELFTEST+ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST 146,768 · the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build
118,680; aarch64 the same 32 undefined functions and 17 variables. **Cost**: ≈ 471 production code lines (426 in the B6
section, the token helper, `zfs_wd_idq3`'s net, `zfs_open`, the two syscall arms) = **21,504 B** of the binding build
(130,720 → 109,216) — against § h's 500 ≈ 20 KB: **−6 % lines, +8 % bytes** (≈ 45.7 B a line); the op list's B6 ops
+154 lines in the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build only.
**The op list** (`zfs_wt_b6`, before the file-data ops; `b6_ops` in the smoke — every success a `zfs-expect.py` op):
`agnos9` 21 ops (mkdir b6, create + a 7-byte write, mkdir -p b6/p/q/r + a 200,000-byte file in it, a symlink in the
root's layout 4, b6empty, b6/many + 9 files: **microzap 512 → 1,024 B**; REFUSED: a 145-byte target, AO_EXCL on
hello.txt, a 50-byte name); `agnoswr` 14 (the root's b6 / a.txt / sym; rs4k's b6d / c.bin + 9,000 B; utf8, dnode
(dnodesize=auto: one slot), quota/b6q, b6empty; REFUSED: a symlink in rs4k (no layout), creates in ro / insens / formd
(read-only datasets) and the fat-ZAP `fat/`); `agnostank` 62 (REFUSED mkdir in the snapshotted root; coff/kid: b6 + 56
files — the 55th lands in object 64, **dnode block 2 of 3: the meta-dnode grew past its `dn_maxblkid`** — and b6's
microzap **512 → 4,096 B**; REFUSED a symlink and a 50-byte name; b6e, w.txt + 100 B); `agnosfull` / `agnoslogs` 4 each
(b6, a.txt + 10 B, b6e; REFUSED a symlink: no layout). Each prints `zfs-wtest: D|F|L` (held to the expected manifest)
and where it went (`object N, dnode block B of M, parent microzap S B`); the wfail lane refuses all.
**zfs-write-smoke PASS 16/16, OpenZFS verify 7/7 — 32 steps each** (30 min 46 s; the guest 7 lanes): host per rw lane
space / uacct (the `obj-` counts of every new object) / holes / sa (`zfs-sa-check.py` B6: every created object's
dnode, layout array by REGISTRY name, every SA value — MODE, SIZE, GEN = its txg, UID 0, GID and PARENT its parent's,
the four times from the op clock, LINKS, PROJID, the ACL — and a FreeBSD twin's layout NUMBER, attribute array, FLAGS
and ACL bytes; every pre-existing parent's SIZE / LINKS / MTIME / CTIME / FLAGS, the rest unchanged: 18 / 9 / 59 / 3 /
3 objects, all twinned); the guest's A5 (object rows == header, fills), B-manifest (the exact expected manifest),
B-dirsize (strict: every agnos directory size == entries + 2), **B-stat** (new: OpenZFS's `stat(1)` of every created
object) and **B-acl** (new: its `getfacl` == a FreeBSD twin's), B-acct (incl. the uid-0 object count), C — FreeBSD
**appends to an agnos file** (`/agnos9/b6/new.txt`, `agnoswr`'s `b6/a.txt`, …), **`rmdir`s an agnos directory**
(`b6empty`), renames the agnos tree and round-trips it (C-rt), D destroys everything with `freeing` / `leaked` 0; `uspace`
7/7. **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS** (zfsx exit 95 — clauses 114–126: mkdir, AO_CREAT|AO_EXCL + write, AO_EXCL refused, AO_CREAT
opens an existing file, stat 0100644 / 040755 with nlink and size, a symlink refused where the dataset has no layout and
on the read-only root, a 50-byte name refused, AO_CREAT|AO_TRUNC on a new name, getdents of the new directory; ten txgs,
T0 + 10 = 108; OpenZFS 32/32 on `main@ring3`, B-stat / B-acl 3 objects). **zfs-smoke PASS 8/8** (G1 byte-identical;
2 min 8 s). **check.sh 38/38**.
**Commit hold** (QEMU/KVM, the kernel's timestamps, a create's begin → its plan line, one txg each): ashift9 NVMe
211–221 ms, AHCI 250–261, usb-storage 246–255; wr 214–219; main 225–276 (the 56-file batch: the microzap rewritten whole
each time, up to 4 KiB × 2 copies); full / logs 253–281 (the first full run's figures). Every txg of the final run's lanes (creates, writes, nulls): ashift9 210–2,076 ms (one ≈ 2 s outlier, the host's `fdatasync` of a freshly written image; its variants 208–243 NVMe, 246–432 AHCI, 298–638 USB), wr 237–764, wr@u0 218–856, main 216–441, full 212–291, logs 204–225.
**Negative proofs** (scratch mutants of `zfs_write.cyr` via cyim, the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` kernel built, the source restored
— sha256 checked — after each; lane `ashift9`, `ZWS_SPACE=advisory ZWS_CONTINUE=1`): **N1** the parent's SIZE not bumped
→ host `sa` red (5 new directories and `/agnos9`), guest **B-dirsize, B-stat** red, then D-root (OpenZFS's `rm -rf`:
"Directory not empty") and D-zdb; **N2** a wrong SA layout number (layout + 1) → host walk (the symlink unreadable), D/L
lines, `sa` red; guest **A5** (zdb `-ddddd` SIGABRT), B-manifest, B-acct, C, D red; **N3** an ACL count off by one
(DACL_COUNT 4, 3 ACEs) → host `sa` red — and the guest was GREEN in all 31 steps: stat, the manifest, zdb, scrub and the
round trip never read an ACL; with the new **B-acl** step FreeBSD's `getfacl` PANICS the guest kernel ("acl_from_aces:
a_type is 0xffff") — red in phase B (k.56); **N4** the new objects' `obj-` deltas skipped → host `uacct` red (`obj-0`
26 vs the recount 44), guest **H, B-acct** (objused − visible objects 1 → −17), D-root (the uid-0 object count wraps
negative), `uspace` red; **N5** a dnode slot reused (an allocated slot taken, the zero check gone: the first mkdir
overwrote `hello.txt`'s dnode) → the op list's own lines (every later create in b6 fails), the walk, `uacct`
(`ds_referenced` off by 1,536: the orphaned blocks), `sa` red; guest **H, A3, A4** ("leaked space: vdev 0, offset
0x1003e00, size 512"), A5, B-manifest, B-stat, B-acl, B-acct, C, D red (`ZWS_SPACE=advisory` now sends a FAILED rw
lane's image to the guest as well, so a mutant that breaks the op list's own lines still shows its guest phases).
**Found and fixed on the way**: the smoke's commit-hold figure did not start a txg at a create's begin (it read up to
5.3 s on `wr`); phase C's journal line printed `rmdir <dir>` twice (`${_cempty:-…}`); a create-only lane (`logs`) called
`zfs-orig.py` with no path (now skipped); N3's blind spot (B-acl). Open for B7+: k.50–k.56.

**B6 REVIEW (2026-10-11, 7 findings — all minor; all confirmed, all fixed).** (1) **The trivial-ACL masks were right for
0644 / 0755 only** (WRITE_NAMED_ATTRS with w, and in every class): `zfs_wc_mask` is now `acl_trivial_access_masks`'
(owner base `0x1E0198`, w = `0x6` everywhere), a mode needing `zfs_acl_chmod`'s allow0 / deny entries is refused by name
(`zfs_wc_trivok`), and the host oracle derives its ACL from OpenZFS's C (allow0 / deny1 / deny2 + the three ALLOWs)
instead of repeating agnos's formula — the two agree on all 64 trivial modes of 512 (an exhaustive host check), and on
0644 / 0755 byte for byte with the fixtures' FreeBSD objects; § f.1, k.55. (2) **A 2 KiB objset on a `project_quota`
pool**: FreeBSD grows it to 4 KiB in memory at open, so its creates carry PROJID — such a create (and any on an objset <
2 KiB) is refused by name (`dse +104` bit 64); § e.3, § f.1, k.60. (3) **Object and project quotas** are enforced only
once `os_flags` carries `USEROBJACCOUNTING_COMPLETE` / `PROJECTQUOTA_COMPLETE` (`zfs_wd_idq3`, bits 16 / 32), as
`zfs_id_overobjquota` / `_overblockquota`; k.59. Measured on the r5 rw images: every head objset is 4 KiB with
`os_flags` 0x7 (ashift9 1, wr 15, main 20, full 1, logs 1), so (2) and (3) change nothing a fixture reaches. (4) **A
microzap parent past 128 KiB** is refused under its own § a.3 name, judged on the parent dnode BEFORE the name lookup
the reader cannot serve, and `zfs_open` now sends every failed `AO_CREAT` lookup to `zfs_wcreate` (which creates only
where its own no-follow lookup says absent) — so no create refusal is unnamed; the reader gap itself is k.57. (5) **A
symlink target holding a NUL** is refused by name (`zfs_wcreate`) and in `ext2_symlink`. (6) **`open`#7 truncated a
relative name to 254 bytes** and opened (created) the truncated name — refused now (`syscall.cyr`; ext2 since 1.54.11,
ZFS since B6; CHANGELOG). (7) **Coverage**: ashift9's op list makes `b6/l144`, a 144-byte target — the 320-byte bonus
exactly full (sa_find_sizes' last fit; FreeBSD reads it back in B-manifest, B-stat, B-acl) — beside the 145-byte
refusal; fixture **RECIPE 5** puts one FreeBSD symlink in `agnostank/custom`, so zfsx's `symlink`#63 SUCCEEDS from ring
3 (`b6/sym`, read back by `lstat` / `readlink` / `stat`), the no-layout refusal moves to `coff/kid`, and new clauses
refuse a NUL target and the 266-byte relative path (127–130); every refused ZFS verb logs `zfs: <verb> refused (<why>)`
(`zfs_wr_say`; k.58) and the harness holds zfsx's 14 refusals to `EXPECT_REFUSED` by NAME and order; and
`zfs-sa-check.py`'s ring-3 form (`--ops --t0 --clock`) holds every object zfsx made or wrote to zfs_mknode's values and
a FreeBSD twin with the PRODUCTION clock (`ntp_now`) inside the run's wall-clock window, one value per op, GEN = its
txg. **Found on the way:** (a) **the main lane's room depended on the fixture build's luck** — agnos allocates only in
metaslabs with a space map (k.46), and r4's main had 8 of 15 (metaslab 1 with a single 4 KiB block), the first r5 build
7: the lane's ~330 txgs (≈ 0.27 MB deferred each for the boot, ≈ 95 MB) ran out at the 244th of the burst's 250 writes
(`no space: the metaslabs with a space map are full`). RECIPE 5 SPREADS main — a fill to the slop in a scratch dataset,
synced, destroyed, `zpool wait -t free` — so the export leaves a space map on every metaslab it touched: **15 of 15**,
held ≥ 12 by the recipe's promise check (`oracle_main_objects`); (b) wr's zvol minor-node wait 30 → 120 s (the first r5
build died "never appeared" at host load 14 on 16 cores); (c) the ring-3 harness first wrote its sa-check manifest as
`<lane>.expect` in the verify dir, which replaces the verify's `.ops` path — B-stat / B-acl SKIPPED and the run still
passed: the manifest now lives outside it, and the harness requires `B-stat PASS` and `B-acl PASS`; (d) image-layout's
frame scan (a bare `48 81 EC` search) read a `call` rel32 of `0xFFEC8148` as a 6 MB frame and refused the
ZFS_SELFTEST+ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST build — now anchored on cycc's prologue (2,374 of 2,374 frames), anything else explained
as a call / jmp displacement or failed; a 5,168 B frame still fails (9) (CHANGELOG). **Fixture** RECIPE 5 →
**`r5-adef67c14321-b583baf8`** (payload: zfsx clauses 114–130; built in 384 s). **Builds** (image-layout headroom to the
`0x400000` wall): plain **188,088 B** · TEST+KTEST 127,440 · **RING3_SELFTEST 106,232 (binding)** · KTEST+THREAD+KSTACK
126,840 · ZFS_SELFTEST+ZFS_WRITE_SELFTEST 143,792 · the `ZFS_WRITE_OPS` build 115,520; aarch64 the same 32 undefined
functions and 17 variables. **Gates (full):** zfs-write-smoke **PASS 16/16, OpenZFS verify 7/7 — 32 steps each** (30 min
07 s; the guest 641 s; ashift9 B-stat / B-acl 19 objects incl. `b6/l144`, main 59, wr 9, full / full@pre / logs 3;
`main`'s burst 1,024,000 B in 250 txgs on the spread image); **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS** (zfsx exit 95; the 14 refusals by
name and in order; `sa` ring-3 form: 4 created objects + 1 written file, 7 op times in the window, all twinned; OpenZFS
32/32 on `main@ring3`, B-stat / B-acl 4 objects incl. the symlink, C appending to agnos's `b6/new.txt`; T0 286 + 11 =
297; 3 min 40 s); **zfs-smoke PASS 8/8** (G1; 2 min 4 s); **check.sh 38/38**. **Negative proofs** (scratch mutants via
cyim, the source restored — sha256 checked — after each): ring 3 — the NUL refusal's NAME changed → zfsx still exits 95
and the harness goes **red on the refusal log** (line 12: "symlink target is bad" ≠ the expected name); the old 254-byte
copy restored → zfsx **exit 130** (the kernel had created `b6/relative-path-past-254-bytes`); host — `zfs-sa-check.py`'s
ring-3 form against the PASSING image with a window in the past → red on every stamped time, with two ops swapped in the
op file → red on GEN (0x6b / 0x6c); image-layout with a 5,120 B local → (9) red (`sub rsp, 0x1430`); the ACL helper and
`zfs_wc_trivok` against the oracle over all 512 modes → 0 disagreements (64 trivial). Not proven by a mutant: the
objset-growth refusal, the `*_COMPLETE` gates and the `large_microzap` pre-check — no fixture reaches them (every head
objset 4 KiB with `os_flags` 0x7; no quota; no microzap past 128 KiB). Open for B7+: k.57–k.61.

**B7 gate record (2026-10-11) — UNLINK / RMDIR / RENAME / LINK in microzap directories** (`zfs_write.cyr` § "B7"
and § "B7 (design k.68)"; § e.2 / f.2–f.5 "(B7, as built)", `zfs.md` § 13). `unlink`#30, `rmdir`#10 (SIZE 2),
`rename`#31 (in one directory or across, over an existing name of the same kind — the target removed in the same txg —,
a directory's PARENT and both parents' LINKS, the cycle walk up the PARENTs, a rename onto its own object no txg) and
`link`#32 (LINKS + 1, never a directory; k.15's stale PARENT) — one txg each through B4's dataset phase: the microzap
chunk zeroed, the parent's SIZE / LINKS / MTIME / CTIME / FLAGS; an object whose last link goes is FREED (its dnode slot
zeroed, every block killed through B5's kill list as `ZWU_FREED`, the user accounting by the commit's own dnode diff) or,
too big for one txg or holding an xattr directory, put on the DELETE_QUEUE (LINKS 0; k.10); every kill decides free or
DEFER in one place (`zfs_wd_defer`: gang, dedup, any block while `block_cloning` / `fast_dedup` is active → `free_bpobj`
+ `$FREE`, D15) — B5's deferred gang truncation lands; D11's GEN check on every ZFS fd, directory fds included; EXDEV
across datasets, the dataset root / a graft / a virtual parent / a directory with a dataset below refused, IMMUTABLE /
APPENDONLY / NOUNLINK refused, every refusal named (§ a.3). **Fixture**: the payload (zfsx clauses 131–145) re-keyed it,

**B8 review record (2026-10-12) — a self-review at the cut's resume: no defect, one record corrected.** A
function-for-function re-read of `zfs_wenc.cyr` § "B8" against OpenZFS 2.4.2 — `zap_leaf.c` (`zap_leaf_array_free`'s
in-order free list, `zap_leaf_transfer_entry`'s copy-then-free — value, name, entry —, `zap_leaf_split`,
`zap_entry_create` / `_update` / `_remove`, `zap_leaf_rehash_entry`'s cd order), `zap.c` (`zap_expand_leaf`,
`zap_grow_ptrtbl`, `zap_table_grow` / `_store` / `_load`, `zap_put_leaf_maybe_grow_ptrtbl`, `fzap_add_cd` /
`fzap_update` / `fzap_remove`) and `zap_micro.c` (`mzap_upgrade`; `zap_add`'s and `zap_update`'s upgrade triggers —
`mze_canfit_fzap_leaf` on an ADD only): equivalent, with two orderings that cannot reach a committed byte — a microzap
add of an EXISTING name answers EEXIST before `mze_canfit_fzap_leaf` (OpenZFS can upgrade first and then fail the add),
and a fat add's EEXIST skips `zap_put_leaf_maybe_grow_ptrtbl`; agnos drops a failed op's txg either way (and the ZPL
never adds an existing name: `zfs_dirent_lock` first). The kernel glue: every resolver address used inside its lifetime
(`zfs_zf_rblk`'s cache slot read before the next block access; the header and the leaves an op writes dirty, so stable),
both operand names (2 × 256 B) bounded at 255 bytes before their copy (`zfs_wu_arg`, `zfs_wcreate`), `zfs_wz_urs`'s key
buffer (24 B ≥ `obj-` + 16 hex digits), every failure inside a ZAP op leaving its txg to be dropped by the caller
(**WRONG** — the maybe-grow swallowed a failed switch, and an accounting ZAP's shape refusal fell through to the commit:
the B8 review's findings, fixed below, k.88 / k.89), the accounting reservation after k.87 (a) — no defect. The two negatives the first run never read (M2, M4) were read: both
red where the plan said (above). **Record corrected**: k.87 (f) said no zdb or OpenZFS step sees a zeroed salt — M5 on
disk shows zdb's path walk does (C-k15). One misnomer left as is: `wr`'s rename target
`f1-renamed-to-a-name-of-fifty-two-bytes-…` is 54 bytes (the comments say 54).

**B8 review fixes (2026-10-12) — five findings, all confirmed, all fixed** (k.85 corrected, k.86 reworded, k.88–k.90;
§ g's post-review measurement). **(1, major) A failed SWITCH was swallowed** — confirmed by reading `zfs_zf_grow`
(`zt_blks_copied` written before the free loop, the switch after it) and `zfs_zf_maybe` (the grow's result dropped),
and by mutant N1 below (the old maybe-grow restored): the op answers 0 where the free failed. Fixed: the last call frees the
old table first (`zfs_zf_kill`: every block or a failure) and then writes the whole header; the maybe-grow ignores
only `ZZ_NOSPC` / the new `ZZ_NOGROW`, any other failure being the op's (its txg dropped); `zt_blks_copied` past the
table is `ZZ_CORRUPT` (k.88 a). The MOS: a growth of either kind refused before any change (`zfs_zf_gok`), the write
gate refusing an external or mid-grow log ZAP (k.85, k.88 b). **(2, minor) The switch's frees** — confirmed by
arithmetic (N old blocks dirtied at 16 KiB each against 1,441,792 B) and on disk: `fz`'s switch txg (350) dirtied 28
blocks before the fix, **24** after (the four old table blocks killed through the kill list, no slot), its extents
(113), alloc (230,400 B), free (194,048 B) and `dsu` (−23,552) identical. Bound now ≈ 1,600 old blocks (k.88 c);
production's salt unpredictable (k.88 d). **(3, minor) Accounting ZAP shapes** — confirmed by reading `zfs_wz_urs`'s
`cap == 0xFFFF && zfs_wz_kind() == 1` (a refusal fell through to `return 0`); refused at open and at admission now
(k.89). **(4, minor) The fat accounting ADD** — confirmed (no gate reached `zfs_wd_uqapply`'s fat add); the boot's
`uafat` selftest (k.90 a; zfs-smoke requires its line on main / ashift9 / logs: `220 new keys … leaves 1->10, nlevels
1->2, dirtied 278528 B of 7340032 reserved, reversed to T-1 ok`). **(5, minor)** — confirmed (the four arms' comments,
slot 8 set and read nowhere, `zfs_wc_fsalt` / `zfs_wc_lnew` / `zfs_wz_up` outside `#ifdef ZFS_WRITE_OPS`); fixed
(k.90 b, c). **Record corrected**: the B8 review record's "every failure inside a ZAP op leaving its txg to be dropped"
(above). **Builds**: § g. **Gates (full, on the final source — sha256 `cb10b2f9…` `zfs_wenc.cyr`, `d8369aec…`
`zfs_write.cyr`):** zfs-write-smoke **PASS 17/17, OpenZFS verify 8/8 — 33 steps each** (67 min 26 s; the guest 774 s; one firmware VOID retried; h9 green on every rw lane — `fz` the same 11 fat ZAPs, 86 leaves, 9,477 entries; its switch txg 350 24 dirty blocks, 4 fewer, its extents, allocations and frees the same); **zfs-ring3-smoke PASS** (zfsx exit 95; 26 txgs 286..311; 28 refusals by
name and in order; h9 on `main@ring3` — its directories now under RDRAND salts —; OpenZFS 33/33; 4 min 51 s);
**zfs-smoke PASS 8/8** (`uafat` on main, ashift9, logs; G1; 2 min 5 s); **check.sh 38/38** (2 min 6 s) (gate 37: **974** of 974
cases, floor 972 → 974); aarch64 the same 32 / 17. **Commit hold** (§ k.8's measure): (median / max, ms) ashift9 221 / 250, AHCI / USB 338 / 561 · 307 / 505, wr / wr@u0 426 / 2,126 · 434 / 529, main 486 / 635, full / full@pre 285 / 413 · 240 / 323, logs 220 / 225, fz 262 / 1,837 — `fz`'s switch txg 229 ms (224 before the fix); every lane's median 5–35 % above the B8 run's, the null-txg lanes and the ones that never reach the changed code alike, on a host the two parallel tracks' QEMU held at load ≈ 11: the maxima are the contention (`fz`'s 1,837 ms rename was 232 ms in the B8 run), not this cut. **Negative proofs**
(cyim mutants, each restored byte-identical and sha256-checked): **N1** the maybe-grow swallows again → gate 37: the
switch case `op 250 (kind 0) returned 0, want -1` (the review's repro, now a red); **N2** `zt_blks_copied` written
before the free (the old order) → gate 37: the second failing add `returned -4, want -1` (the copied-past-the-table
guard catches the header OpenZFS would have misread); **N3** the maybe-grow does not ignore `ZZ_NOGROW` → gate 37: the
no-grow case's update `returned -8, want 0`; **N4** `zfs_wz_urs`'s kind refusal falls through again → zfs-smoke main,
ashift9, logs `b3a: FAIL … uafat: a fat accounting ZAP with zap_flags admitted`; **N5** the fat accounting reservation
dropped → the same three `uafat: the adds dirtied more than zfs_wz_urs reserved`; **N6** `zfs_zf_kill` kills a COPY of
the BP (the old table freed, its L1 still naming it) → `fz` (`ZWS_SPACE=advisory`): host **h7** `agnosfz: object 3:
dn_used 304128 != Σ dsize of its tree 353280`, and FreeBSD's kernel PANICKED importing the pool (`ms_allocatable:

snapshot-owned BP is deadlisted, not dropped; a kill list's deadlisted BP records no FREE extent; `zfs_wd_okbp` refuses
an encrypted BP before branch B can take it; the dataset phase is the only place a materialisation runs (no MOS block is
dirty before a commit); every new refusal is named (`zfs_wno`).

log's destruction reserved first, per distinct dnode block (k.115; k.117 (4)). (3) The destroy's backstop counted the
dataset phase twice and refused every destroy on wr's 2 MiB write txgs — `zfs_wl_kroom` (k.117 (6)). (4) The smoke's
hold measure charged a drain's txgs and the op list's stats to the commit (k.117 (7); a measurement). (5) At the
resume: `zfs.md` § 16 named `zfs_wh_plan` for the dirty-first histogram read that `zfs_wh_need` does (doc
only). **The run was stopped by the operator at its very end (≈ 6 h) and resumed:** the source was confirmed free of

`6e82ccc1…`) had passed 17/17 with OpenZFS 8/8 too. N3 alone was re-run (its function changed); the other negatives'
mutated code is unchanged.
